# <span class="deva-large">चुरादिगणः</span> <span class="iast-large">Curādigaṇaḥ</span>

<div class="dict-page-header">
  <span class="badge-count">509 Wurzeln</span>
  <div class="header-nav-links">
    <a href="./09-kryadi" class="nav-prev">← Vorheriger Gaṇa</a>
  </div>
</div>

> **Charakteristik:** Kausativ- und Nennverbklasse mit ṇic-Infix (-aya-) und obligatorischer Stammsteigerung.  
> **Musterbildung:** `चुर् (cur) → चोरयति (corayati)`

---

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0001">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चुर्</span>
    <span class="iast">cur</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0001</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">स्तेये</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(steye)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to steal</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">चुरँ</span> (curam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2563</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · mspsetu · chittoor3 · common · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; स्तेये चोरयतीति स्यात्, चुरेश्छेदे चुरेदिति &#x27; (श्लो. 151) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0002">

      <span class="iast-artha">(smṛtyām)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to contemplate,to think,to discuss,to remember, to worry</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">चितिँ</span> (citim̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2564</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · mspsetu · common · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; चिन्तयेत् । स्मृत्याम् , चेतति संज्ञाने , तत्र चेतयते णिचि &#x27; (श्लो. 94) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">दर्शनाङ्कनयोः</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(darśanāṅkanayoḥ)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to observe,to see,to look, to mark, to point, to deliberate</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">लक्षँ</span> (lakṣam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2564</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अङ्कनम् = चिन्हम् &#x27; इति क्षीरस्वामी । &#x27; आलोचनार्थाण्णिचि लक्षयेत, स्याल्लक्षयेल्लक्षयतेङ्कनार्थात् &#x27; (श्लो. 182) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">जलँ</span> (jalam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2564</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> लज &#x27; इत्येके इति तत्त्वबोधिनी । बालमनोरमायां तु &#x27; लज &#x27; इत्येव पाठः । &#x27; जल &#x27; इति पाठान्तरम् । मा.धा.वृत्तौ तु &#x27; जल &#x27; इत्येव पाठः ।</div>

      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">अवस्पन्दने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(avaspandane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to enact, to dance, to act, to show</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">नटँ</span> (naṭam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2565</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अयं धातुः न णोपदेशः । &#x27; णोपदेशास्तु अनर्दनाट् ....&#x27; इति पर्युदासात् । अतः &#x27; उपसर्गात् समासेपि ...&#x27; (8.4.14) इति सोपसर्गे णत्वं न । अवस्पन्दनं नाट्यम् । &#x27; अवस्यन्दने &#x27; इत्येके ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0041">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शठ्</span>
    <span class="iast">śaṭh</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0041</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">असंस्कारगत्योः</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(asaṃskāragatyoḥ)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to cheat,to fraud, to leave unfinished, to leave unattended, to ill-behave</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">शठँ</span> (śaṭham̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2567</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> संस्कारगत्योः &#x27; इति मैत्रेयकाशकृत्स्नकातन्त्रादयः । &#x27; संस्कारगत्यो: अनृतभाषणे गतौ च &#x27; इति काशकृत्स्नः । &#x27; आलस्ये च &#x27; इति धा.का.व्या व्याख्यायाम् । (3.16) &#x27; आलस्ये गत्यसंस्कृतसंस्कृते &#x27; इति वोपदेवः । &#x27; गत्यसंस्कारधात्वर्थे शाठयेच्छ्वाठयेदिति &#x27; (श्लो. 81) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

      <span class="iast-artha">(hiṃsābalādānaniketaneṣu)</span>

    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">तुजिँ</span> (tujim̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2567</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>

  </div>

    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> यौ वा णौ अपचारणे छदत इत्येकम्, द्वितीयं छदेत् (छदन्), अन्यत् छादयति, छदेदिति पुनः स्यादूर्जनेर्थे पितः । &#x27; यत्तु च्छन्दयतीति णौ नुमि पदं, तत् संवृताविष्यते &#x27; (श्लो. 113) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

</div>

      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">भूषायां हर्षे च</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(bhūṣāyāṃ harṣe ca)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to adorn,to decorate, to be happy</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">मडिँ</span> (maḍim̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2567</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; क्वचित् कोशे अत्र हर्षे च इति न पठ्यते । पाठशुद्धिरत्र कीदृशी इति न विद्मः &#x27; इति कृदन्तरूपमाला । &#x27; भूषार्थे मण्डतीति स्यात्तत्र मण्डयतीति णौ &#x27; (श्लो. 84) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0077">

    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> कालयति - द्रावयति इत्यर्थः । &#x27; कालयति - प्रेरयति &#x27; इति तत्त्वबोधिनी । दन्तोपघातं कालयति &#x27; हिंसार्थायाम् &#x27; (3.4.48) इत्यत्र । &#x27; कलेः कालयति क्षेपे, गतिसंख्यानयोर्द्वयोः । कलयेत् शब्दसंख्यानविषये कलने शपि &#x27; (श्लो. 155) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> वज &#x27; इति केचित् । &#x27; मार्ग &#x27; इति पृथग् धातुरिति बहूनां पक्षः । तथा हि तत्त्वबोधिनी - &quot; वज मार्ग । वाजयति । मार्गयति । केचित् तु मार्ग इति न धात्वन्तरं , किन्तु वज इति एक एव धातुः मार्गसंस्कारे गतौ च इति व्याचख्युः &quot; इति ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0110">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शुल्क्</span>
    <span class="iast">śulk</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0110</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">अतिस्पर्शने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(atisparśane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to tax, to pay tax, to produce goods</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">शुल्कँ</span> (śulkam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2567</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; सर्जने &#x27; इति स्वामी । &#x27; भाषणे &#x27; इति काशकृत्स्नः । किं च अन्यत्र चुरादौ &#x27; शुक्ल सर्जने &#x27; इति च तत्र दृश्यते । &#x27; अतिसर्जने &#x27; इति केचित् । &#x27; सर्जने , वर्जने (वर्णने) इति वोपदेवः । &#x27; सर्जनम् = आर्जनम् । वर्जनभाषणयोश्च द्रुमे इति धा.का.व्याख्या ।(3 - 23)</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0112">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षम्प्</span>
    <span class="iast">kṣamp</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0112</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">क्षान्त्याम्</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(kṣāntyām)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to endure,to tolerate, to forgive, to shine</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षपिँ</span> (kṣapim̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2567</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; क्षम्पेः क्षम्पयति क्षान्त्यां प्रेरणे क्षपयेदिति &#x27; (श्लो. 136) इति देवः । &#x27; क्षप प्रेरणे &#x27; इति धातुः चुरादौ अदन्ते अस्ति इति एतस्मात् श्लोकात् अवगम्यते । सिद्धान्तकौमुद्यां तु न दृश्यते इति कृदन्तरूपमाला ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> गतौ च । चकारात् कृच्छ्रजीवने इति दौर्गा: &#x27; इति स्वामी । &#x27; बिले गतौ तङ्के &#x27; इति वोपदेवः । &#x27; श्वभ्रिः बिलार्थे च द्रुमे &#x27; इति धा.का. व्याख्या ।(3.24)</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0118">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्ञप्</span>
    <span class="iast">jñap</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0118</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">ज्ञाने ज्ञापने च</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(jñāne jñāpane ca)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to know, to understand, to teach, to educate, to explain, to clarify, to make happy, to hit, to sharpen</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्ञपँ</span> (jñapam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2567</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · मित् · common · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; जानातीति श्नि सिद्ध्येत्, ज्ञपयति तु पुनर्मारणादौ घटादेः णौ (चौ) मित्त्वेपीदमेव &#x27; ज्ञप मिद् &#x27; इति पदं ज्ञापने मारणादौ । तेनार्थाज्ज्ञापनेर्थे ज्ञपयतिपदवज्ज्ञापयेदित्यपि स्याद् उक्तस्योक्ति: &#x27; णिचश्चे &#x27; (1.3.74) त्युदितविहतये ज्ञापयेज्ज्ञा नियोगे &#x27; (श्लो. 8) इति देवः । मित्त्वविषये केचन विशेषांशाः कृदन्तरूपमालायां विवृताः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">यम्</span>
    <span class="iast">yam</span>

      <span class="iast-artha">(pariveṣaṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to keep under control, to nourish, to nurture, to serve food</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">यमँ</span> (yamam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2568</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · मित् · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; यमेरुपरमे यच्छेत् यमयेत् परिवेषणे &#x27; (श्लो. 148) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &quot; क्वचित् धातुपाठेस्य अदृष्टत्वेपि नायमप्राणिकः इति मन्तव्यम् । &#x27; मिदचोन्यात्परः &#x27; इति सूत्रे कैयटेन उपन्यस्तत्वात् &#x27; मर्तो मर्तं मर्चयति द्वयेन &#x27; इति प्रयोगदर्शनाच्च &quot; इति तत्त्वबोधिनी ।</div>

    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> म्लेच्छनम् अपशब्दनम् इति धातुवृत्तिः । &#x27; म्रक्षणेप्ययम् । तच्च तैलादिना अभ्यञ्जनम् &#x27; इति तत्त्वबोधिनी । &#x27; म्लेच्छने म्रक्षयेत् म्रक्षेत् सङ्घाते &#x27; (श्लो. 171) इति देवः ।</div>

      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">अव्यक्तायां वाचि</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(avyaktāyāṃ vāci)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to speak inarticulately, to speak uncultured, to talk rude, to talk indescent</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">म्लेछँ</span> (mlecham̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>

  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">पूर्वनिकेतने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(pūrvaniketane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to dwell, to reside, to stay, to invite</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुर्दँ</span> (gurdam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> केचिदिह गर्ज गर्द शब्दे, गर्ध अभिकाङ्क्षायाम् इति पठन्ति &#x27; इति सिद्धान्तकौमुदी । &#x27; पूर्वनिकेतनम् - पूर्वव्यवहारः इत्येके &#x27; इति धातुकाव्यव्याख्या । पूर्वनिकेतनम् - आद्यनिवासः &#x27; इति क्षीरस्वामी । &#x27; पूर्व &#x27; इति धात्वन्तरम् इति केचित् ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

      <span class="iast-artha">(rakṣaṇe mokṣaṇe ca)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to protect,to release</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">जसिँ</span> (jasim̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; मोक्षणे च &#x27; इति केचित् । &#x27; मोक्षणे जस्यतीति स्याण्णावत्रार्थे तु जंसयेत् &#x27; (श्लो. 188) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

      <span class="iast-artha">(roṣe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to be angry</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">रुषँ</span> (ruṣam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> रुट &#x27; इति क्षीरस्वामी । &#x27; शपि रोषति हिंसायाम्, रोषे रुष्यति रोषयेत् &#x27; (श्लो. 179) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0192">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चित्</span>
    <span class="iast">cit</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0192</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ātmanepada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">सञ्चेतने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(sañcetane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to think to perceive, to meditate, to remember, to memorize</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">चितँ</span> (citam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> आकुस्मीयाः आत्मनेपदिनः &#x27; इति सिद्धान्तकौमुदी । सञ्चेतनम् - मूर्च्छावस्थानिवृत्त्युत्तरकालिकं ज्ञानम् । &#x27; स्मृत्यां , चेतति संज्ञाने तत्र चेतयते णिचि &#x27; (श्लो. 94) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0193">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दंश्</span>
    <span class="iast">daṃś</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0193</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ātmanepada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">दंशने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(daṃśane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to bite,to sting</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">दशिँ</span> (daśim̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; भासार्थः &#x27; इति क्षीरस्वामी । &#x27; दंशने दशतीति स्यात् तत्र दंशयते णिचि । भाषणे दंशयेत् ... &#x27; (श्लो. 164- 165) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0194">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दंस्</span>
    <span class="iast">daṃs</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0194</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ātmanepada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">दर्शनदंशनयोः</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(darśanadaṃśanayoḥ)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to see, to bite,to sting</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">दसिँ</span> (dasim̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; दंशयेन्मोक्षणे दस्येत् क्षये दंसयते णिचि । दंशदर्शनयोः, दासेर्दाने दासति दासते &#x27; (श्लो. 186 - 187) - देवः । &#x27; दस &#x27; इत्येके ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0197">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">डिप्</span>
    <span class="iast">ḍip</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0197</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ātmanepada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">सङ्घाते</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(saṅghāte)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to collect, to bring together, to pile up, to heap</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">डिपँ</span> (ḍipam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; सङ्घाते डेपयेतीति, क्षेपे डिपति डिप्यति । डेपयेदिति चत्वारि णिचि शे श्यनि णौ डिपेः &#x27; (श्लो. 134) इति देवः ।  &quot; डबि डिबि &#x27; इति चान्द्र: । विडम्बयति । आडम्बरः । डिम्बयति । डिम्बम् - शस्त्रकलहः इति क्षीरस्वामी । प्रदर्शितप्रयोगानुसारेण डबिडिबिधात्वोः अपि पाठः समीचीनः इति प्रतिभाति &quot; इति कृदन्तरूपमाला ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0198">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तन्त्र्</span>
    <span class="iast">tantr</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0198</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ātmanepada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">akarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">कुटुम्बधारणे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(kuṭumbadhāraṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to support the family, to govern, to lead</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">तत्रिँ</span> (tatrim̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> चान्द्राः धातुद्वयमिति मत्वा &#x27; कुटुम्बयते इत्युदाहरन्ति &#x27; इति सिद्धान्तकौमुदी ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0199">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मन्त्र्</span>
    <span class="iast">mantr</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0199</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ātmanepada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">गुप्तपरिभाषणे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(guptaparibhāṣaṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to consult, to advise, to discuss privately</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">मत्रिँ</span> (matrim̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; गुप्तभाषणे &#x27; इत्येव प्रायशः पाठः । &#x27; मन्त्रि &#x27; इति कुत्रचित् दृष्टः पाठः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0200">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">स्पश्</span>
    <span class="iast">spaś</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0200</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ātmanepada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">ग्रहणसंश्लेषणयोः</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(grahaṇasaṃśleṣaṇayoḥ)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to take, to join, to unite</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">स्पशँ</span> (spaśam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; स्पृश &#x27; इति जैन - शाकटायन - हेमचन्द्रादयः । &#x27; ग्रहणे श्लेषणे चार्थे चुरादौ स्पाशयेत णौ &#x27; (श्लो. 167) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0201">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तर्ज्</span>
    <span class="iast">tarj</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0201</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ātmanepada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">तर्जने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(tarjane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to hate, to insult, to ignore, to blame, to criticize, to frighten</span>
    </div>

    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">गूरँ</span> (gūram̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; गुरते गू(गु)रयेतेति शे णावुद्यमने तङि । श्यनि गूर्यत इत्येवं गतिहिंसनयोस्तङि &#x27; (श्लो. 152) इति देवः । केचित् &#x27; गुरी &#x27; इति पठन्ति । अन्ये &#x27; गूरी &#x27; इति पठन्ति ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0218">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शम्</span>
    <span class="iast">śam</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0218</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ātmanepada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">आलोचने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(ālocane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to publish, to advertise, to explain, to clarify</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">शमँ</span> (śamam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · मित् · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> आलोचने शामयते शाम्यत्युपशमे श्यनि &#x27; (श्लो. 149) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0219">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">लक्ष्</span>
    <span class="iast">lakṣ</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0219</span>

    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">आलोचने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(ālocane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to criticize</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">लक्षँ</span> (lakṣam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> आलोचनार्थात् णिचि लक्षयेत , स्याल्लक्षयेत् लक्षयतेङ्कनार्थात् &#x27; (श्लो. 182) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0220">

    </div>
  </div>

      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">अवक्षेपणे निन्दने च</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(avakṣepaṇe nindane ca)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to insult, to criticize, to blame, to hate</span>
    </div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0224">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भल्</span>
    <span class="iast">bhal</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0224</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ātmanepada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">आभण्डने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(ābhaṇḍane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to analyze, to argue</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">भलँ</span> (bhalam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> आभण्डनम् - निरूपणम् । &#x27; मार्जनम् &#x27; इति धातुकाव्यम् ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0225">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कूट्</span>
    <span class="iast">kūṭ</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0225</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ātmanepada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">आप्रदाने अवसादने च</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(āpradāne avasādane ca)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to avoid giving, to cheat, to do indistinctly, to collect</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">कूटँ</span> (kūṭam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; अवसादने &#x27; इत्येके । &#x27; कूटयेताप्रसादे णौ कूटेः दाहे तु कूटयेत् &#x27; (श्लो. 75) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0226">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुट्ट्</span>
    <span class="iast">kuṭṭ</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0226</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">

      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">प्रतापने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(pratāpane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to heat</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुट्टँ</span> (kuṭṭam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0227">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">वञ्च्</span>
    <span class="iast">vañc</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0227</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ātmanepada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">प्रलम्भने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(pralambhane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to cheat</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">वन्चुँ</span> (vancum̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> प्रलम्भनम् = मिथ्याफलाख्यानम् । &#x27; वञ्चयेत प्रलम्भने &#x27; (श्लो. 48) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0228">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">वृष्</span>
    <span class="iast">vṛṣ</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0228</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ātmanepada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">akarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">शक्तिबन्धने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(śaktibandhane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to be impregnated, to show great courage, to be capable of bearing offsprings</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">वृषँ</span> (vṛṣam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> शक्तिबन्धनं प्रजाननसामर्थ्यं शक्तिबन्धः च । &#x27; शक्तौ वर्षयते सेके वर्षेत् स्नहे तु वर्षते &#x27; (श्लो. 176) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0229">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मद्</span>
    <span class="iast">mad</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0229</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ātmanepada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">तृप्तियोगे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(tṛptiyoge)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to please, satisfy</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">मदँ</span> (madam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> तृप्तिशोधने , शोधनं - सम्पत्तिः &#x27; इति क्षीरस्वामी । &#x27; तृप्तिशोधने - तर्पणशुद्धौ &#x27; इति पुरुषकारमाधवाभ्यां क्षीरस्वामिसम्मतत्वेन उपात्तं वाक्यम् । तत् तु मुद्रिते क्षीरतरङ्गि तरङ्गिणीकोषे न दृश्यते &#x27; इति कृदन्तरूपमाला । &#x27; स्तुत्यादौ मन्दते हर्षे माद्येत् मादयते णिचि &#x27; (श्लो. 112) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0230">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दिव्</span>
    <span class="iast">div</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0230</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ātmanepada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">akarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">परिकूजने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(parikūjane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to irritate, to cause pain, to mourn to grieve</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">दिवुँ</span> (divum̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; अर्दने देवयेद्, दीव्येत् क्रीडादौ, परिकूजने । देवयेतेदितो दिन्वेद्, देवेर्देवेत देवने &#x27; (श्लो. 160-161) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0231">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गृ</span>
    <span class="iast">gṛ</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0231</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ātmanepada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">विज्ञाने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(vijñāne)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to know, to understand</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">गृ</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; ज्ञाने गारयते, गिरेन्निगरणे, शब्दे गृणाति त्रयं युक्तं, ग्रो गरतीति सेकविषये ह्रस्वान्तधातोः शपि &#x27; (श्लो. 33) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0232">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">विद्</span>
    <span class="iast">vid</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0232</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ātmanepada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">चेतनाख्याननिवासेषु</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(cetanākhyānanivāseṣu)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to improve body, to know, to understand</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">विदँ</span> (vidam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; विवादेषु &#x27; इति क्षीरस्वामी । &#x27; वेदने &#x27; इत्येके । &#x27; विद - वेदनाख्यान - परिवादनेषु &#x27; इति न्यासे पाठः । &#x27; निपातनेषु इति धनपालशाकटायनौ &#x27; इति पुरुषकारः । &#x27; विन्दते विन्दतीत्येवं लाभे, वेदयते णिचि &#x27; (श्लो. 111) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0233">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मान्</span>
    <span class="iast">mān</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0233</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ātmanepada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">akarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">स्तम्भे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(stambhe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to boast, to be egoistic, to be proud, to stabilize</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">मानँ</span> (mānam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> स्तम्भे मानयते ज्ञाने मन्यते मनुते पदम् &#x27; (श्लो. 125) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">स्तम्भे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(stambhe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to boast, to be egoistic, to be proud, to stabilize</span>
    </div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0235">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">यु</span>
    <span class="iast">yu</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0235</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ātmanepada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">जुगुप्सायाम्</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(jugupsāyām)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to insult, to blame, to criticise</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">यु</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> यावयेत जुगुप्सायाम्, मिश्रणे यौति, बन्धने । युनाति च युनीते च ... &#x27; (श्लो. 24) इति देवः । &#x27; युजु कुत्सायाम् &#x27; इत्येके । &#x27; योजयते &#x27; इति क्षीरस्वामी ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0236">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुस्म्</span>
    <span class="iast">kusm</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0236</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ātmanepada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">akarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">कुत्सितस्मये</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(kutsitasmaye)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to smile inappropriately, to smile with an insult</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">कुस्मँ</span> (kusmam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &quot; कुस्म नाम्नः वा &#x27; इति गणसूत्रम् । &#x27; कुस्म &#x27; इति धातुः कुत्सितस्मये वर्तते इति तस्य तात्पर्यम् । अथवा - &#x27; कुस्म &#x27; इति प्रातिपदिकम् । ततः धात्वर्थे णिच् &quot; इति सिद्धान्तकौमुदी । विषयेस्मिन् केचन विशेषांशाः कृदन्तरूपमालायाम् उक्ताः दृश्यन्ते ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0237">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चर्च्</span>
    <span class="iast">carc</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0237</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">अध्ययने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(adhyayane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to learn, to study</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">चर्चँ</span> (carcam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; अध्ययने चर्चयेत् चर्चतीति तु । हिंसायां तर्जने शे तु तथैव परिभाषणे &#x27; (श्लो. 54) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0238">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">बुक्क्</span>
    <span class="iast">bukk</span>

      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">भषणे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(bhaṣaṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to bark</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">बुक्कँ</span> (bukkam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> णिचि बुक्कयतीति स्याद्भाषणे शपि बुक्कति &#x27; (श्लो. 42) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0239">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शब्द्</span>
    <span class="iast">śabd</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0239</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">आविष्कारे, भषणे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(āviṣkāre, bhaṣaṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to utter, to sound, to explain</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">शब्दँ</span> (śabdam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> उपसर्गात् परः शब्दधातुः आविष्कारे वर्तते इत्यर्थः । प्रतिशब्दति । प्रतिश्रुतिमाविष्करोति इत्यर्थः । अनुपसर्गाच्च आविष्कारे इत्येव । चाद्भाषणे ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0240">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कण्</span>
    <span class="iast">kaṇ</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0240</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">निमीलने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(nimīlane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to wink</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">कणँ</span> (kaṇam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; एकनेत्रनिमीलने एव अयं प्रसिद्धः स्वभावात् &#x27; इति तत्त्वबोधिनी । &#x27; कणेः कणति शब्दार्थे काणयेर्णौ निमीलने &#x27; (श्लो. 90) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0241">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जम्भ्</span>
    <span class="iast">jambh</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0241</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">नाशने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(nāśane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to kill, to destroy</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">जभिँ</span> (jabhim̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; जृम्भणे जम्भते यामे जम्भेन्नाशे तु जम्भयेत् &#x27; (श्लो. 145) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0242">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">सूद्</span>
    <span class="iast">sūd</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0242</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">क्षरणे आस्रवणे आप्रवणे घाते च</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(kṣaraṇe āsravaṇe āpravaṇe ghāte ca)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to drip, to ooze, to kill, to try to kill, to wound</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">षूदँ</span> (ṣūdam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> आस्रवणे &#x27; इति क्षीरतरङ्गिणी । &#x27; आप्रवणे &#x27; इति केचित् । &#x27; घाते &#x27; इति केचित् ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0243">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जस्</span>
    <span class="iast">jas</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0243</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">

      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">ताडने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(tāḍane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to kill, to strike, to hit, to ignore</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">जसुँ</span> (jasum̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> हिंसताडनयोर्धात्वोः उदितोर्जासयेदिति &#x27; (श्लो. 189) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

    </div>

      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">बन्धने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(bandhane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to lockup, to bind, to hang till death</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">पशँ</span> (paśam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0245">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">अम्</span>
    <span class="iast">am</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0245</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">

      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">रोगे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(roge)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to be ill,to be sick, to have stomach ache</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">अमँ</span> (amam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> गत्यादावमतीति स्याद्रोगे स्यादामयत्यमे: &#x27; (श्लो. 147) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0246">

    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">चटँ</span> (caṭam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0247">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">स्फुट्</span>
    <span class="iast">sphuṭ</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0247</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">akarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">भेदने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(bhedane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to cut, to split, to crack, to open, to trouble</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">स्फुटँ</span> (sphuṭam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> स्फोतयेत्स्फोटतीति द्वे भेदे विशरणे क्रमात् &#x27; (श्लो. 71) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0248">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">घट्</span>
    <span class="iast">ghaṭ</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0248</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">सङ्घाते</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(saṅghāte)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to crush, to chop, to gather</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">घटँ</span> (ghaṭam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> भेदने च &#x27; इति धातुकाव्यव्याख्या । &#x27; हन्त्यर्थाच्च &#x27; (ग.सू.) - नवगण्याम् उक्ताः अपि हन्त्यर्थाः स्वार्थे णिचं लभन्ते इत्यर्थः &#x27; - कौमुदी । &#x27; हन हिंसागत्योरिति हनधातोः हिंसा गमनं च अर्थः । एतदर्थकाः ये धातवः भ्वादिषु नवसु गणेषु पठिताः ते सर्वे चुरादौ पठिता: प्रत्येतव्याः इत्यर्थः । ततश्च तेभ्यः स्वार्थे णिजपि पक्षे भवति इति फलितम् &quot; - बालमनोरमा । &#x27; घाटयेद् घण्टयेद् सङ्घभाषयो: घटते मितः &#x27; (श्लो. 70) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0249">

      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">मर्दने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(mardane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to rub, to massage</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">दिवुँ</span> (divum̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; अर्दने &#x27; इति क्षीरस्वाम्यादयः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0250">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">अर्ज्</span>
    <span class="iast">arj</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0250</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">प्रतियत्ने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(pratiyatne)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to earn, to do business, to prepare</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">अर्जँ</span> (arjam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> प्रतियत्नः = गुणाधानम् - तत्त्वबोधिनी । &#x27; अयमर्थान्तरेपि । द्रव्यमर्जयति &#x27; इति कौमुदी । &#x27; प्रतियत्नेर्जयेदर्जेरर्जतीत्यर्जने पदम् &#x27; (श्लो. 63) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0251">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">घुष्</span>
    <span class="iast">ghuṣ</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0251</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">विशब्दने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(viśabdane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to announce, to speak loudly</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">घुषिँर्</span> (ghuṣim̐r)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; शब्दनं शब्देन स्वाभिप्रायप्रकाशनम् । ततः अन्यत् विशब्दनम् &#x27; इति मा.धा.वृत्तिः । &#x27; घुषेः विशब्दनादौ णौ घोषयत्यविशब्दने । घोषतीतीदितो घुंषेद् घुंषते कान्तिकर्मणि &#x27; (श्लो. 169, 170) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0252">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रन्द्</span>
    <span class="iast">krand</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0252</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">akarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">सातत्ये</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(sātatye)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to call out</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्रन्दँ</span> (krandam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> आङः क्रन्दः - सातत्ये &#x27; इति पाठे, आङः परः क्रन्दतिः सतताह्वाने, सततरोदने चार्थे गम्यमाने णिचं लभते इत्यर्थः । अन्ये तु &#x27; आङ्पूर्वो घुषिः क्रन्दसातत्ये &#x27; इत्याहुः । आघोषयति &#x27; इति द्वेधा व्याख्या ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0253">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">लस्</span>
    <span class="iast">las</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0253</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">akarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">शिल्पयोगे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(śilpayoge)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to be skilled, to be expert, to know art</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">लसँ</span> (lasam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> क्रियाकौशलं शिल्पम् । &#x27; शिल्पयोगे = कौशले इत्यर्थः &#x27; इति बालमनोरमा । &#x27; शिल्पयोगे = भ्रमादिना तक्ष्णोतीत्यर्थः &#x27; इति क्षीरस्वामी । &#x27; लश &#x27; &#x27; लष &#x27; इति पाठान्तरम् अपि दृश्यते ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0254">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तंस्</span>
    <span class="iast">taṃs</span>

      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">अलङ्करणे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(alaṅkaraṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to adorn, to decorate</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">तसिँ</span> (tasim̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> तसि भूष अलङ्कारे &#x27; इति दुर्गसम्मतः पाठः इति स्वामी । प्रायेणायम् अवपूर्वः । &#x27; तस्यत्युपक्षयार्थे णौ , अलङ्कारे तु तंसयेत् &#x27; (श्लो. 189) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0258">
  <div class="skt-header-line">

  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">नियोगे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(niyoge)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to order</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">ज्ञा</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> स्वभावदयं धातुराङ्पूर्वः । नियोगः = प्रेरणम् । &#x27; जानातीति श्नि सिद्ध्येत्, ज्ञपयति तु पूनार्मारणादौ घटादेः णौ (चौ), मित्त्वेपीदमेव ज्ञपनमिति ( ज्ञप मिदिति) पदं ज्ञापने मारणादौ । तेनार्थाज्ज्ञापनेर्थे ज्ञपयतिपदवत् - ज्ञापयेदित्यपि स्यात् ; उक्तस्योक्तिः - &#x27; णिचश्चे &#x27; (1-3-74) त्युदितविहतये; ज्ञापयेत् &#x27; ज्ञा नियोगे &#x27; । (श्लो. 8) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0259">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भज्</span>
    <span class="iast">bhaj</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0259</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">विश्राणने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(viśrāṇane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to give, to donate, to cook, to make readm to prepare food, to segregate</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">भजँ</span> (bhajam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> विश्राणनम् = दानम् । &#x27; सेवायां भजते भजेदिति भजेः विश्राणने भाजयेद्..भाजयेद&#x27; (श्लो. 65) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0260">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शृध्</span>
    <span class="iast">śṛdh</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0260</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">प्रहसने प्रसहने च</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(prahasane prasahane ca)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to tolerate, to insert, to disrespect, to defeat</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">शृधुँ</span> (śṛdhum̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &quot; शृध अप्रहसने । अप्रहसनम् = अमर्षः । प्रहसने = अभिभवे इत्येके &quot; इति स्वामी । &#x27; प्रहसनम् = विद्रावः इति गोविन्दभट्ट: &#x27; इति शब्दकल्पद्रुमे । &#x27; प्रहसने &#x27; इति शाकटायनकाशकृत्स्नौ । &#x27; प्रहसनम् = अभिभवः &#x27; इति धातुकाव्यव्याख्या । (3.40) &#x27; हिक्कादौ शर्धयत्यस्य शृधेः प्रहसने णिचि &#x27; (श्लो. 118) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0261">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">यत्</span>
    <span class="iast">yat</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0261</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">akarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">निकारोपस्कारयोः</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(nikāropaskārayoḥ)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to cause pain, to kill, to irritate, to disallow,  to stop</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">यतँ</span> (yatam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> यत्नो वा प्रैषो वा निकारः &#x27; इति बालमनोरमा । &quot; क्रियानिघण्टौ - &#x27; यत्ने प्रैषे निराकारे पादपे चाप्युपस्कृतौ । नित्योयं धान्यधनयोः प्रतिदाने &#x27; इत्युक्तम् । अस्यार्थः - &#x27; यत्नाद्यर्थेषु चतुर्षु यतधातुं प्रयुञ्जीत । निसः परभागे चेत्प्रयुज्यते तदायं धान्यधनयोः प्रतिदाने च वर्तते &#x27; इति । ऋणं निर्यातयति । प्रतिददाति इत्यर्थः &quot; इति तत्वबोधिनी । &#x27; यतते तु प्रयत्नार्थे, निराकारे तु यातयेत् &#x27; (श्लो. 96) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

    </div>

  <div class="skt-body">

      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">विशेषणे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(viśeṣaṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to characterise, to honor, to remove, to separate</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">अन्चुँ</span> (ancum̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> व्यावर्तने इत्यर्थः । विशेषणम् = व्यावर्तनम् । &#x27; गतियाचनयोरञ्चत्यञ्चते णौ विशेषणे । अञ्चयत्यञ्चतीत्येकं गतिपूजनयोरपि &#x27; (श्लो. 50) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

      <span class="en-artha">— to mix, to unite</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुदँ</span> (mudam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> (उदा - मोदयति सक्तून् घृतेन ) &#x27; संसर्गे णिचि मोदयेदिति भवेद्धर्षे पुनर्मोदते &#x27; (श्लो. 113) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0269">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रस्</span>
    <span class="iast">tras</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0269</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">धारणे ग्रहणे वारणे च</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(dhāraṇe grahaṇe vāraṇe ca)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to catch, to steal, to take forcefully, to disobey, to frighten</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रसँ</span> (trasam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; ग्रहणे &#x27; इत्येके, &#x27; वारणे &#x27; इत्यन्ये । &#x27; भाषणे त्रंसयेत् त्रंसेर्धारणे त्रासयेत् णिचि । त्रसति त्रस्यतीत्येवमुद्वेगार्थे तु वा श्यनि &#x27; (श्लो. 191) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0270">

      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">उञ्छे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(uñche)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to pick, to glean</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">उँध्रसँ</span> (um̐dhrasam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> ध्रस्नाति ध्रासयत्युञ्छे पदे तु श्नि णिचि ध्रसेः &#x27; (श्लो. 190) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0271">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">उध्रस्</span>
    <span class="iast">udhras</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0271</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">उञ्छे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(uñche)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to pick, to glean</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">उध्रसँ</span> (udhrasam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0272">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुच्</span>
    <span class="iast">muc</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0272</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">प्रमोचने मोदने च</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(pramocane modane ca)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to free,to liberate,to release,to loosen,to abandon,to grant,to give money</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">मुचँ</span> (mucam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> मोदने च &#x27; इति मा.धा.वृत्तौ सिद्धान्तकौमुद्यां च दृश्यते, न क्षीरतरङ्गिण्यादिषु । &#x27; कल्कने मुञ्चते मुञ्चेर्मोक्षे मुञ्चति मुञ्चते । प्रमोचने चुरादेर्णौ मोचयत्येष न त्व्ऌदित् &#x27; (श्लो. 47) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0273">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">वस्</span>
    <span class="iast">vas</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0273</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">स्नेहच्छेदापहरणेषु</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(snehacchedāpaharaṇeṣu)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to love, to cut, to destroy, to kidnap, to desire</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">वसँ</span> (vasam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; उपहरणेषु इति वा पाठः &#x27; इति धातुकाव्यम् । (3.41) &#x27; अवहरणेषु &#x27; इति कातन्त्रशाकटा शाकटायनचन्द्रादयः । &#x27; अवहननेषु इति पाठान्तरम् &#x27; इति क्षीरतरङ्गिण्याम् । &#x27; वस्यति स्तम्भने वस्ते छादने वसयेत् वसेत् । निवासे वासयेत्, स्नेहे वासयत्युपसेवने &#x27; (श्लो. 187 - 188) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0274">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चर्</span>
    <span class="iast">car</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0274</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 105">S. 105</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">संशये</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(saṃśaye)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to doubt</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">चरँ</span> (caram̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> संशये चारयेत् गत्यां चरति &#x27; (श्लो. 153) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0275">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">च्यु</span>
    <span class="iast">cyu</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0275</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
