    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">सहने हसने च</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(sahane hasane ca)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to endure, to tolerate, to laugh</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">च्यु</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> हसने च &#x27; इत्येके । &#x27; च्युस &#x27; इत्येके च्योसयति ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0277">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भू</span>
    <span class="iast">bhū</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0277</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">अवकल्कने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(avakalkane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to mix, to think</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">भू</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; भुवः अवकल्पने &#x27; णिच् स्यात् । अवकल्कनं मिश्रीकरणम् इत्येके । चिन्तनमित्यन्ये । मा.धा.वृत्तौ कृदन्तरूपमालायां च अस्य धातोः अर्थस्य विषये विस्तृता चर्चा विहिता दृश्यते । पुरुषकारः वदति - &#x27; मूलशुद्धिः अत्र कीदृशी इति न विद्मः &#x27; इति । &#x27; सत्तायां भवति, प्राप्तौ णिचि भावयते तङि । भवते शपि तत्रैव भावयत्यवकल्कने &#x27; (श्लो. 3) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0278">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृप्</span>
    <span class="iast">kṛp</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0278</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">akarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">अवकल्कने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(avakalkane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to mix, to think, to imagine, to paint</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">कृपँ</span> (kṛpam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; कल्पते शपि सामर्थ्ये, कल्पयत्यवकल्कने । अदन्तस्य कृपेर्णौ तु दौर्बल्ये कृपयेदिति &#x27; (श्लो. 135) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0279">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रस्</span>
    <span class="iast">gras</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0279</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">ग्रहणे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(grahaṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to seize, to catch, to steal</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रसँ</span> (grasam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; आ स्वदः सकर्मकात् &#x27; (ग.सू. 201) - इत आरभ्य आस्वदीयाः सकर्मकाः ज्ञेयाः इत्यर्थः । &#x27; अस्य धातोः आस्वदीयत्वेन &#x27; आ स्वदः सकर्मकात् &#x27; इति वचनात् कर्मसंज्ञकार्थान्वयबोधकत्वे एव णिच् भवति । अकर्मकत्वे तु न इति बोध्यम् &#x27; इति कृदन्तरूपमाला । ग्रासयति फलम् ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0280">

    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">धारणे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(dhāraṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to wear, to obey</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">पुषँ</span> (puṣam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> पोषयत्याभरणम् । &#x27; धारणे पोषयेत्पुष्टौ पुष्येत्पुष्णाति पोषति &#x27; (श्लो. 171) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0281">

      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">विदारणे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(vidāraṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to break, to cut, to slice, to do pieces</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">दलँ</span> (dalam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> विदारणे शपि दलेद्दालयेदिति णौ दलेः&#x27; (श्लो. 159) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">पटँ</span> (paṭam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>

  </div>
</div>

    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">पुटँ</span> (puṭam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0284">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">लुट्</span>
    <span class="iast">luṭ</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0284</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>

      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">भाषायाम्</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(bhāṣāyām)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to speak, to talk, to shine, to glow</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">लुटँ</span> (luṭam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> णौ लोटयति भाषार्थे लुट्येल्लोटति लोटने &#x27; (श्लो. 73) - देवः ।</div>

</div>

      <span class="iast-artha">(bhāṣāyām)</span>

    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">लघिँ</span> (laghim̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अयं धातुः भासार्थकः सकर्मकः इति पारायणम् &#x27; इति क्षीरस्वामी । &#x27; भाषणे लङ्घयेत् शोषे लङ्घेत् गत्यां तु लङ्घते &#x27; इति देवः । (श्लो. 45)</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0292">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रंस्</span>
    <span class="iast">traṃs</span>

    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">भाषायाम्</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(bhāṣāyām)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to speak, to talk, to shine, to glow</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">त्रसिँ</span> (trasim̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> त्रिसि &#x27; इति केषाञ्चित् मतम् । त्रिसिपिसी इदुपधौ । अदुपधौ इत्येके &#x27; इति बालमनोरमा । किन्तु मूले (कौमुद्यां) &#x27; त्रसि &#x27; इत्येव दृश्यते । &#x27; भाषणे त्रसयेत् त्रंसेर्धारणे त्रासयेण्णिचि &#x27; (श्लो. 191) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0312">
  <div class="skt-header-line">

    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>

    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">भाषायाम्</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(bhāṣāyām)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to speak, to talk, to shine, to glow</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">वृतुँ</span> (vṛtum̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2571</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> भासार्थः &#x27; इति क्षीरस्वामिशाकटायनादयः । &#x27; दीप्तौ &#x27; इति वोपदेवः । &#x27; तथा क्षीरस्वामी - भासादीप्तिरर्थे येषां ते भासार्थाः &#x27; इति मा.धा.वृत्तौ । क्षीरतरङ्गिण्यां तु अयं पाठः न उपलभ्यते । &#x27; वर्तयेद् भाषणार्थे णौ वर्तने शपि वर्तते &#x27; (श्लो. 96) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">शीकँ</span> (śīkam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>

      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">वयोहानौ</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(vayohānau)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to grow old</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">जॄ</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> जृणाति जीर्यति जरत्येकार्थे जारयत्यपि &#x27; (श्लो. 39) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0348">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रिच्</span>
    <span class="iast">ric</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0348</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">वियोजनसम्पर्चनयोः</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(viyojanasamparcanayoḥ)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to mix, to join, to tie, to separate, to spread, to take laxative</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">रिचँ</span> (ricam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; वियोजनसंयमनयोः इति मुकुटः &#x27; इति अमरसुधा । सम्पर्चनं - मिश्रीकरणम् । अभिधानवैचित्र्यात् धातोरस्य परस्परविरुद्धार्थकत्वम् । &#x27; रिचिर् विरेचने रिङ्क्ते रिणक्ति श्ने, वियोजने । सम्पर्चने च यौ वा णौ रेचयत्यपि रेचति &#x27; (श्लो. 52) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0349">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिष्</span>
    <span class="iast">śiṣ</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0349</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">असर्वोपयोगे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(asarvopayoge)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to retain, to save</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">शिषँ</span> (śiṣam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> शिषोसर्वोपयोगे यौ शेषयेत् शेषतीत्युभे । लुङ्यभावादङोशेषीत् ( &#x27; अशेषीत् &#x27; इत्यस्य आयुक्तता दैवव्याख्याने पुरुषकारे एव उक्ता - &#x27; अत्र अशेषीत् इति अयुक्तम् । &#x27; शिषिम् पिषिम् &#x27; इति अनिट्कारिकाप्रामाण्यात् शिषिमात्रस्य अनिट्त्वेन &#x27; शल इगुपधात्..&#x27; (3.1.45) इति क्से सति &#x27; अशिक्षत् &#x27; इत्येव युक्तत्वात् इति । ) शिनष्टीति विशेषणे &#x27; (श्लो. 178 - 179) इति देवः । &#x27; अयं विपूर्वोतिशये वर्तते &#x27; इति कौमुदी ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0350">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तप्</span>
    <span class="iast">tap</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0350</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">दाहे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(dāhe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to become hot, to glow, to burn, to be jealous</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">तपँ</span> (tapam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> सन्तापेर्थे तपेत् दाहे तापयेत् तपते तपेत् &#x27; (श्लो. 131) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0351">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तृप्</span>
    <span class="iast">tṛp</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0351</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">तृप्तौ सन्दीपने प्रीणने च</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(tṛptau sandīpane prīṇane ca)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to be satisfied, to be happy to satisfy, to make happy</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">तृपँ</span> (tṛpam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> सन्दीपने &#x27; इति क्षीरस्वामी । &#x27; प्रीणने &#x27; इति कविकल्पद्रुमे । &#x27; तृप्तावन्यतरस्यां णौ स्यातां तर्पति तर्पयेत् &#x27; (श्लो. 133) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0352">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">छृद्</span>
    <span class="iast">chṛd</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0352</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">सन्दीपने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(sandīpane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to ignite, to set on fire</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">छृदीँ</span> (chṛdīm̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; छृद &#x27; इति केचित् । &#x27; चृप छृप दृप सन्दीपने । चर्पयति, छर्पयति &#x27; इति कौमुदी । &#x27; छृदी सन्दीपने वा णौ छर्दयेच्छर्दतीत्युभे । छृन्ते छणत्तीति पदे दीप्तिदेवनयोः श्नमि &#x27; (श्लो. 115) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">मोक्षणे हिंसायां च</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(mokṣaṇe hiṃsāyāṃ ca)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to set free, to  leave, to kill, to destroy, to hurt</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रथँ</span> (śratham̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; हिंसायाम् &#x27; इति केचित् । &#x27; दौर्बल्ये श्रथयेत् प्रयत्नविषये स्याच्छ्राथयेत् मोक्षणे । यौ तु श्राथयति श्रथत्यपि च वा णौ श्रन्थयेत् श्रन्थति । सन्दर्भे श्नि विमोचनार्थविषये श्रथ्नात्यथ श्रन्थते, शैथिल्ये.. &#x27; (श्लो. 101) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0361">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मी</span>
    <span class="iast">mī</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0361</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">गतौ</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(gatau)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to go</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">मी</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> गतौ मनने &#x27; इति क्षीरस्वामी । &#x27; मीञो मीनाति मीनीते हिंसायां मीयते ङितः । मिनोति मिनुते स्वादेः , गतौ मयति माययेत् &#x27; (श्लो. 16 - 17) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0362">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रन्थ्</span>
    <span class="iast">granth</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0362</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">बन्धने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(bandhane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to tie, to bind</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रन्थँ</span> (grantham̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; ग्रन्थ &#x27; इति पाठः युक्तः इति कृदन्तरूपमाला । तथा हि - &#x27; चुरादौ ग्रन्थ बन्धने, ग्रन्थ सन्दर्भे इति द्वौ धातू आधृषीयौ पठ्येते । देवश्लोकात् पुरुषकारवाक्यात् च &#x27; ग्रथ बन्धने &#x27; इति पाठः एव साधुः इति प्रतिभाति । अतः एव धातुकाव्यव्याख्याने (3.50) नारायणभट्टेनापि ग्रथ बन्धने इत्येव अनुवादः क्रियते । तेन सिद्धान्तकौमुद्यादिषु दृष्टः ग्रन्थ बन्धने इति पाठः अयुक्तः इति गम्यते । &#x27; तथा हि देवः - ग्रथ्नातीति तु सन्दर्भे ग्रन्थयेत् ग्रन्थतीति यौ । कौटिल्ये ग्रन्थते ग्रन्थे: ग्राथयेत् ग्रथति ग्रथे: &#x27; (श्लो. 17) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0363">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शीक्</span>
    <span class="iast">śīk</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0363</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">आमर्षणे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(āmarṣaṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to touch, to be quiet, to tolerate, to suffer</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">शीकँ</span> (śīkam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; सेके आमर्षे च &#x27; इति वोपदेवप्रभृतयः । तत्तु &#x27; शीकृ सेचने &#x27; इति भ्वादिधातोः सम्पद्यत इति व्यर्थमेव । &#x27; मर्षणे शीकयेत् शीकेत् सेचने शपि शीकते &#x27; (श्लो. 42) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0364">

      <span class="iast-artha">(āmarṣaṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to tolerate, to suffer, to be eager, to be hasty, to be intolerant</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">चीकँ</span> (cīkam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>

      <span class="en-artha">— to kill, to detroy, to irritate, to cause pain</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">अर्दँ</span> (ardam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> (स्वरितेत्)  &#x27; अर्दयेदर्दतेर्देद्यौ हिंसनेर्दति याचने &#x27; (श्लो. 103) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

    </div>

      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">पूजायाम्</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(pūjāyām)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to be worthty, to be eligible, to have merit</span>
    </div>

    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> पूजनेर्हति चार्हयेत् &#x27; (श्लो. 197) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0368">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">सद्</span>
    <span class="iast">sad</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0368</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>

    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">पद्यर्थे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(padyarthe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to attack, to climb, to go</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">षदँ</span> (ṣadam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> णिजभावे अनिट् अयं धातुः।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0369">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शुन्ध्</span>
    <span class="iast">śundh</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0369</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">akarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">शौचकर्मणि</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(śaucakarmaṇi)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to cleanse, to purify</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">शुन्धँ</span> (śundham̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> शौचकरणे &#x27; इति क्षीरस्वामी । &#x27; शुन्ध्येत् शुन्धते शुन्धेद् यौ वा णौ शौचकर्मणि &#x27; (श्लो. 117) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0370">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">छद्</span>
    <span class="iast">chad</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0370</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">अपवारणे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(apavāraṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to remove, to get rid of, to hide, to keep out of sight</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">छदँ</span> (chadam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> स्वरितेत् । &#x27; बलीभवति प्राणीभवति अपवारयति वा इत्यर्थः &#x27; - कौमुदी । &#x27; यौ वा णावपवारणे छदत इत्येकं , द्वितीयं छदेत्, (छदन्) अन्यत् छादयति, छदेदिति पुनः स्यादूर्जनेर्थे मितः । यस्तु च्छन्दयतीति णौ नुमि पदं तत् संवृताविष्यते &#x27; (श्लो. 113) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0371">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">जुष्</span>
    <span class="iast">juṣ</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0371</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">परितर्कणे परितर्पणे च</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(paritarkaṇe paritarpaṇe ca)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to think, to wish, to kill, to destroy, to guess, to make happy, to satisfy, to be satisfied</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">जुषँ</span> (juṣam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> परितर्कणम् ऊहः हिंसा वा । &#x27; परितर्पणे &#x27; इत्यन्ये । परितर्पणं परितृप्तिक्रिया । &#x27; जोषयेज्जोषतीत्येते युजादेः परितर्कणे । प्रीतिसेवनयोः इष्टं तुदादेर्जुषते पदम् &#x27; (श्लो. 180 - 181) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0372">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">धू</span>
    <span class="iast">dhū</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0372</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">कम्पने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(kampane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to shake, to move, to decorate</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">धूञ्</span> (dhūñ)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> धूनयति धवति धवते, धुनोति धुनुते, धुनाति च धुनीते । धूनोति धूनुति स्युः पदानि कम्पे, विधूनने धुवति &#x27; (श्लो. 26) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0373">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">प्री</span>
    <span class="iast">prī</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0373</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">तर्पणे कान्तौ च</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(tarpaṇe kāntau ca)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to love, to satisfy, to desire, to wish, to be radiant</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">प्रीञ्</span> (prīñ)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> प्रीङ् प्रीतौ प्रीयतेप्रीणात् प्रीणीते तर्पणे ञितः । अणौ यौ ञित्त्व - साफल्यात् प्रीणयेत् प्रयते प्रयेत् &#x27; (श्लो. 19 - 20) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0374">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रन्थ्</span>
    <span class="iast">śranth</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0374</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">सन्दर्भे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(sandarbhe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to compose, to order, to intertwine</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्रन्थँ</span> (śrantham̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0375">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रन्थ्</span>
    <span class="iast">granth</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0375</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">सन्दर्भे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(sandarbhe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to refer, to reference</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">ग्रन्थँ</span> (grantham̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; ग्रथ्नातीति तु सन्दर्भे ग्रन्थयेत् ग्रन्थतीति यौ । कौटिल्ये ग्रन्थते ग्रन्थे: ग्राथयेत् ग्रथति ग्रथे: &#x27; (श्लो. 97) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0376">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">आप्</span>
    <span class="iast">āp</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0376</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">लम्भने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(lambhane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to obtain, to get, to receive</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">आपॢँ</span> (āpḷm̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; आपयत्यापतीत्यापेः यौ वा णौ लम्भने पदे । व्याप्त्यर्थस्यास्य तु स्वादेराप्नोतीति पदं भवेत् &#x27; (श्लो. 137) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0377">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">तन्</span>
    <span class="iast">tan</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0377</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">श्रद्धोपकरणयोः</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(śraddhopakaraṇayoḥ)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to believe, to have faith, to provide shelter, to help, to sound, to kill, to destroy, to hurt</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">तनुँ</span> (tanum̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> उपसर्गाच्च दैर्घ्ये &#x27; । &#x27; चन श्रद्धोपहननयोः &#x27; इत्येके । चानयति - चनति । &#x27; श्रद्धोपकरणार्थस्य तनेस्तनति तानयेत् &#x27; (श्लो. 127) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0378">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">चन्</span>
    <span class="iast">can</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0378</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">श्रद्धोपहननयोः</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(śraddhopahananayoḥ)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to believe, to have faith, to provide shelter, to help, to sound, to kill, to destroy, to hurt</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">चनँ</span> (canam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0379">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">वद्</span>
    <span class="iast">vad</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0379</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">सन्देशवचने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(sandeśavacane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to inform,to communicate,to speak,to tell,to describe,to utter, to read</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">वदँ</span> (vadam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> स्वरितेत् । अनुदात्तेदित्येके । &#x27; सन्देशने &#x27; इति क्षीरस्वामी मैत्रेयः च । &#x27; भाषणे &#x27; इति हेमचन्द्रजैनकातन्त्रादयः । &#x27; वाक्संदेशयोः &#x27; इति वोपदेवः । &#x27; वचने च इति द्रुमे &#x27; इति धातुकाव्यव्याख्या । (3.51) &#x27; अयम् आत्मनेपदी &#x27; इति शाकटायनः । &#x27; व्यक्तवाचि वदेद्वा णौ वादयेद्वदते वदेत् &#x27; (श्लो. 109) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0380">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">वच्</span>
    <span class="iast">vac</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0380</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">परिभाषणे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(paribhāṣaṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to inform,to communicate,to speak,to tell,to describe,to utter, to read</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">वचँ</span> (vacam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; सन्देशने च &#x27; इति कातन्त्रहेरम्भादयः । &#x27; वक्तीति भाषणे वा णौ वाचयेद्वचतीति वा &#x27; (श्लो. 51) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0381">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मान्</span>
    <span class="iast">mān</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0381</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">पूजायाम्</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(pūjāyām)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to respect, to believe</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">मानँ</span> (mānam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> स्तम्भे तु मानयते &#x27; इति कौमुदी । &#x27; पूजायां मानायेन्मानेद्यौ मीमांसेत तङ्सनोः &#x27; (श्लो. 126) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0382">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">भू</span>
    <span class="iast">bhū</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0382</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ātmanepada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">प्राप्तौ</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(prāptau)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to obtain, to receive, to get, to think</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">भू</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> सत्तायां भवति, प्राप्तौ णिचि भावयते तङि । भवते शपि तत्रैव भावयत्यवकल्कने &#x27; (श्लो. 3) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0383">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्ह्</span>
    <span class="iast">garh</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0383</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">विनिन्दने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(vinindane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to blame, to criticize</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">गर्हँ</span> (garham̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> ण्यन्तस्य ग्रहणे गृहेर्गृहयते तत्रानदन्ताद् गृहेः भूवादेः शपि गर्हते, श्नि तु पदे गृह्णात्यगृह्णीत च । गर्हेर्वा णिचि निन्दनार्थविषये गर्हेत् तथा गर्हयेत् भूवादेः शपि कृत्सनार्थविषये गर्हेर्भवेत् गर्हते &#x27; (श्लो. 194) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0384">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">मार्ग्</span>
    <span class="iast">mārg</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0384</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">अन्वेषणे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(anveṣaṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to investigate, to search, to cleanse, to purify</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">मार्गँ</span> (mārgam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; अन्वेषणेर्थे मृगयेत णौ यौ मार्गेर्भवेन्मार्गति मार्गयेच्च &#x27; (श्लो. 44) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0385">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">कण्ठ्</span>
    <span class="iast">kaṇṭh</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0385</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">akarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">शोके</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(śoke)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to be excited, to mourn, to grieve</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">कठिँ</span> (kaṭhim̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> उत्पूर्वोयम् उत्कण्ठायाम् &#x27; इति कौमुदी । &#x27; अथ कण्ठते । शोके स्यात् कण्ठयेत् कण्ठेत्, कठे स्यात् कृच्छ्रजीवने &#x27; (श्लो. 79) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0386">

    <span class="iast">mṛj</span>

      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">शौचालङ्कारयोः</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(śaucālaṅkārayoḥ)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to cleanse, to purify, to  to decorate</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृजूँ</span> (mṛjūm̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> शौचालङ्कारयोर्वा णौ मृजेर्मार्जति मार्जयेत् &#x27; (श्लो. 60) इति देवः । &#x27; मृजूष् &#x27; इति क्षीरस्वामी ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">तितिक्षायाम्</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(titikṣāyām)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to tolerate</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृषँ</span> (mṛṣam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> स्वरितेत् &#x27; इति कौमुदी । &#x27; क्षमायां यौ मर्षेन्मर्षेत मर्षयेत् &#x27; (श्लो. 173) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0388">

    <span class="deva" lang="sa">धृष्</span>
    <span class="iast">dhṛṣ</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0388</span>

      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">प्रसहने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(prasahane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to conquer, to win, to be eager, to be afraid</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">धृषँ</span> (dhṛṣam̐)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2572</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · णिज्विकल्पः</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> धृषेः प्रसहने यौ स्तां धर्षयेद्धर्षतीत्युभे &#x27; (श्लो. 181) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0392">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">शठ</span>
    <span class="iast">śaṭha</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0392</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">सम्यगवभाषणे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(samyagavabhāṣaṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to ill-speak, to abuse, to tell lies, to keep quiet</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">शठ</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2573</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः ।  &#x27; शठयेत् श्वठयेदेते द्वे सम्यगवभाषणे &#x27; (श्लो. 81) इति देवः । &#x27; सम्यगाभाषणे सम्यग्वचनक्रियायामिति क्षीरस्वामी &#x27; इति पुरुषकारः । क्षीरतरङ्गिण्यां तु &#x27; असम्यगाभाषणे &#x27; इत्येव दृश्यते । &#x27; सम्यगाभाषणे &#x27; इति दुर्गः । &#x27; सम्यग्भाषणे &#x27; इति शाकटायनः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0393">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्वठ</span>
    <span class="iast">śvaṭha</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0393</span>

    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">सम्यगवभाषणे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(samyagavabhāṣaṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to ill-speak, to abuse, to tell lies, to keep quiet</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">श्वठ</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2573</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः ।  &#x27; शठयेत् श्वठयेदेते द्वे सम्यगवभाषणे &#x27; (श्लो. 81) इति देवः । &#x27; सम्यगाभाषणे सम्यग्वचनक्रियायामिति क्षीरस्वामी &#x27; इति पुरुषकारः । क्षीरतरङ्गिण्यां तु &#x27; असम्यगाभाषणे &#x27; इत्येव दृश्यते । &#x27; सम्यगाभाषणे &#x27; इति दुर्गः । &#x27; सम्यग्भाषणे &#x27; इति शाकटायनः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0394">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">पठ</span>
    <span class="iast">paṭha</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0394</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">ग्रन्थे वेष्टने च</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(granthe veṣṭane ca)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to clothe, to knead, to cover, to make parts</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">पठ</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2573</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः । &#x27; वेष्टने &#x27; इति क्षीरस्वामी । &#x27; भाषार्थे पाटयेद् ग्रन्थे पटयेद् गमने पटेत् &#x27; (श्लो. 76) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0395">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">वठ</span>
    <span class="iast">vaṭha</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0395</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">ग्रन्थे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(granthe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to tie, to join</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">वठ</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2573</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः । &#x27; वेष्टे भागे च &#x27; इति वोपदेवः । &#x27; वेष्टने इत्येके &#x27; इति धातुकाव्यव्याख्याने । (3.54) &#x27; वेष्टने इति क्षीरस्वामी &#x27; इति मा.धा.वृत्तौ उक्तम् । क्षीरतरङ्गिण्यां तु &#x27; वट ग्रन्थे ग्रन्थो वेष्टनम् &#x27; इत्येव दृश्यते । &#x27; णावदन्ताद्वेटेर्ग्रन्थे वटयेद् वेष्टने वटेत् &#x27; (श्लो. 77) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0396">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रह</span>
    <span class="iast">raha</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0396</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">

      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">त्यागे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(tyāge)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to leave, to abandon</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">रह</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2573</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः । &#x27; रहयेत् रहति त्यागे गतौ रंहेस्तु रंहति &#x27; (श्लो. 195) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">देवशब्दे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(devaśabde)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to have thunderstorm, to have cloud collision</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">स्तन</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2573</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः । देवशब्दः पर्जन्यगर्जनम् । &#x27; शब्दे च देवशब्दे च स्तनति स्तनयेत्क्रमात् &#x27; (श्लो. 127) - देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0399">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">गद</span>
    <span class="iast">gada</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0399</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">देवशब्दे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(devaśabde)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to have thunderstorm, to have cloud collision</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">गद</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2573</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः । देवशब्दः = पर्जन्यध्वनिः । &#x27; णौ देवशब्दे गदयेत्, व्यक्तवाचि गदेच्छपि &#x27; (श्लो. 110) इति देवः । &#x27; केचित् तु &#x27; गदी &#x27; इति ईदितं धातुं पठन्ति । शाकटयनोपि एवमेव इत्यादिकं सर्वं पुरुषकारादिषु द्रष्टव्यम् &#x27; इति कृदन्तरूपमाला ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0401">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">पष</span>
    <span class="iast">paṣa</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0401</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">गतौ</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(gatau)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to go</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">पष</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2573</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः । &#x27; पष अनुपसर्गात् । गतौ इत्येव । पषयति &#x27; इति सिद्धान्तकौमुदी । &#x27; भ्वादेश्चुरादेश्चानुपसर्गस्य पषेः णिच्यदन्तत्वविधिः ... &#x27; इत्येके । &#x27; पशोनुपसर्गात् &#x27; इति क्षीरस्वामिसम्मतः पाठः । &#x27; अनुपसर्गात् &#x27; इति कथनात् सोपसर्गे न णिच् । &#x27; प्रपषति &#x27; इति उदाहरणम् । उपसर्गाभावे तु णिच् अदन्तता च ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

      <span class="iast-artha">(upadhāraṇe)</span>

    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">शील</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2573</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>

  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0420">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">साम</span>
    <span class="iast">sāma</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0420</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">सान्त्वप्रयोगे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(sāntvaprayoge)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to consolidate, to make happy, to pacify</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">साम</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2573</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> साम सान्त्वने इत्यतीतस्य तु असीषमत् &#x27; इति कौमुदी । अस्मिन् विषये तत्त्वबोधिनी वदति - “ अयं धातुः इतः प्राक् मूलपुस्तके कुत्रापि न दृष्टः । पुस्तकान्तरेषु मृग्यः । केचित् तु साम सान्त्वने इत्यस्य क्वाप्यपठितत्वेपि षान्त्व सामप्रयोगे इति प्राक् पठितमेव । तत्र च षान्त्व सामेति धातुद्वयं प्रयोगे वर्तते । प्रयोगश्च सान्त्वप्रयोगपरः । स च सान्त्वनमेव इत्येवं ग्रन्थकाराशयं वर्णयित्वा स्थितस्य गतिं समर्थयन्ते “ इति । &#x27; सान्त्वप्रयोगः - अकटुभाषणम् &#x27; इति बालमनोरमा । &#x27; सान्त्वने इति क्षीरस्वामी &#x27; इति माधवः । तरङ्गिण्यां तु &#x27; सान्त्वनं = प्रीणनम् &#x27; इत्येव दृश्यते । चान्द्रमते &#x27; सान्त्वप्रयोगे &#x27; इति च तत्रोक्तम् । तदा तु (चान्द्रमते) &#x27; असीसमत् &#x27; ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0421">

  </div>
</div>

    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">पल्पूल</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2573</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0426">
  <div class="skt-header-line">

    <span class="iast">vāsa</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0426</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">उपसेवायाम्</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(upasevāyām)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to make someone stay, to make something fragrant, to burn incense sticks</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">वास</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2573</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः । वस्यति स्तम्भने वस्ते छादने वासयेद्वसेत् । निवासे वासयेत् स्नेहे वासयत्युपसेवने । (श्लो. 187- 188) ( वसेन्निवासे वसयेच्च वस्ते त्वाच्छादने स्तम्भन एव वस्येत् । निवासयत्याच्छदने निवासेः आसेवने वासयतीति वासे ॥) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0427">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">निवास</span>
    <span class="iast">nivāsa</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0427</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">

      <span class="iast-artha">(ācchādane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to cover, to surround</span>
    </div>

  </div>
</div>

  </div>
</div>

  </div>
</div>

    </div>

  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">आमन्त्रणे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(āmantraṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to invite, to call, to sign,  to speak intelligently</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">गुण</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2573</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः । पाठान्तरम् - केत श्रावणे निमन्त्रणे च । केतयति । निकेतयति । कुण गुण च आमन्त्रणे । चकारात् केतति - कौमुदी ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">गतौ</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(gatau)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to go, to change places</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">पद</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2573</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः । आवर्गीयः ( गर्वधातुपर्यन्ताः आत्मनेपदिनः ) । &#x27; श्यनि गत्यर्थे पद्यते पदयेत णौ &#x27; (श्लो. 109) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0441">

    <div class="skt-badges">

      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>

      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">ग्रहणे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(grahaṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to take, to accept</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">गृह</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2573</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः । आवर्गीयः । &#x27; ण्यन्तस्य ग्रहणे गृहेर्गृहयते, तत्रानदन्ताद् गृहेः भूवादेः शपि गर्हते, श्नि तु पदे गृह्णात्यगृह्णीत च । गर्हेर्वा णिचि निन्दनार्थविषये गर्हेत् तथा गर्हयेद् भूवादेः शपि कुत्सनार्थविषये गर्हेर्भवेद् गर्हते &#x27; (श्लो. 194) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

      <span class="iast-artha">(anveṣaṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to investigate, to search, to prey, to kill an animal</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">मृग</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2573</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>

  </div>
</div>

      <span class="en-artha">— to put in thread, to cover with thread</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">सूत्र</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2573</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>

  </div>
</div>

      <span class="iast-artha">(citrīkaraṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to paint, to draw, to create a portrait</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">चित्र</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2574</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः । &#x27; आलेख्यकरणे &#x27; इत्यर्थः । कदाचिद्दर्शने । &#x27; चित्र &#x27; इत्ययमद्भूतदर्शने णिचं लभते । कदाचिद्दर्शने इत्यस्य विवरणम् अद्भूतदर्शने इति &#x27; इति बालमनोरमा । चित्रयति - सिद्धान्तकौमुदी ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

</div>

  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">पदे लक्षणे च</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(pade lakṣaṇe ca)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to mark, to count, to roam around, to limp, to take in lap</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Aupadeśika:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">अन्क</span> (anka)</span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2574</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः । &#x27; अङ्क लक्षण इत्यस्य भवेदङ्कयतीति णौ &#x27; (श्लो. 41) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0475">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">सुख</span>
    <span class="iast">sukha</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0475</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">तत्क्रियायाम्</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(tatkriyāyām)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to make happy, to be happy</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">सुख</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2574</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> “ प्रातिपदिकात् धात्वर्थे बहुलमिष्ठवच्च &#x27; (ग.सू. चुरादौ) इत्यस्य विवरणरूपोयं धातुः । सुखदुःखशब्दयोः &#x27; तत् करोति, तदाचष्टे, तेनाक्रामति &#x27; (ग.सू.चुरादौ) इत्येतेषु अर्थेषु &#x27; णिच् &#x27; भवति । &#x27; सनाद्यन्ताः धातवः &#x27; (3.1.32) इति धातुसंज्ञा “ इति कृदन्तरूपमाला । “ प्रातिपदिकात् धात्वर्थे …&#x27; इत्येव सिद्धे अनयोः पाठः सोपसर्गात् प्रत्ययार्थम् इत्येके । सुखदुःखाभ्यां तत्क्रियायामेव णिच् स्यात् , न तु आचष्टे इत्यस्मिन्नर्थे इत्येतदर्थः पाठः इत्यन्ये “ इति तत्त्वबोधिनी । अत्र तत्क्रिया = सुखनम् । सुखानुकूलः - व्यापारः । &#x27; तत्क्रिया = तदनुभवः &#x27; इति धातुकाव्यव्याख्याने ।(3.62)</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0476">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुःख</span>
    <span class="iast">duḥkha</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0476</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">तत्क्रियायाम्</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(tatkriyāyām)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to make sad, to be sad</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">दुःख</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2574</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः । ( &#x27; तत्क्रिया &#x27; इति पदस्य &#x27; सुख &#x27; धातौ यादृशं विवरणं तादृशमेव अत्रापि ।)</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0477">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रस</span>
    <span class="iast">rasa</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0477</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">आस्वादनस्नेहनयोः</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(āsvādanasnehanayoḥ)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to tastem to love, to have affection</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">रस</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2574</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः । &#x27; शब्दे शपि रसेत् स्नेहास्वादयो रसायेद्रसेः &#x27; (श्लो. 193) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

      <span class="iast-artha">(vittasamutsarge)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to spend, to expense</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">व्यय</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2574</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः । &#x27; वित्तत्यागे व्यययति व्ययति व्ययते गतौ &#x27; (श्लो. 150) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0479">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">रूप</span>
    <span class="iast">rūpa</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0479</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">रूपक्रियायाम्</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(rūpakriyāyām)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to create a shape, to formulate, to create a mental picture</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">रूप</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2574</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः ।  रूपस्य दर्शनं करणं वा रूपक्रिया । रूपक्रिया = राजमुद्रादिरूपस्य करणम् ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0481">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">छद</span>
    <span class="iast">chada</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0481</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">अपवारणे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(apavāraṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to remove, to hide, to conceal</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">छद</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2574</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः । &#x27; यौ वा णौ अपवारणे छदत इत्येकम्, द्वितीयं छदेत्, ( छदन्) अन्यत् छादयति, छदेदिति पुनः स्यादूर्जनेर्थे मितः । यस्तु च्छन्दयतीति णौ नुमि पदं तत् संवृताविष्यते &#x27; (श्लो. 113) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
