    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">प्रेरणे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(preraṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to inspire</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">लाभ</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2574</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः ।  &#x27; प्रेरणे लाभयेल्लाभे प्राप्यर्थे लभते शपि &#x27; (श्लो. 146) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0483">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">व्रण</span>
    <span class="iast">vraṇa</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0483</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">गात्रविचूर्णने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(gātravicūrṇane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to attack, to hurt, to damage someone, to wound</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">व्रण</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2574</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः । &#x27; व्रणेच्छब्दार्थविषये व्रणयेद् गात्रचूर्णने &#x27; (श्लो. 93) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0484">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">वर्ण</span>
    <span class="iast">varṇa</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0484</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">वर्णक्रियाविस्तारगुणवचनेषु</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(varṇakriyāvistāraguṇavacaneṣu)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to explain, to elaborate, to spread, to praise, to glow, to shine</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">वर्ण</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2574</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः ।  वर्णक्रिया वर्णकरणम् । सुवर्णं वर्णयति । कथां वर्णयति । विस्तृणाति इत्यर्थः । हरिं वर्णयति । स्तौतीत्यर्थः । &#x27; रागे विस्तारे प्रशंसायां च &#x27; इति काशकृत्स्नः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0486">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">विष्क</span>
    <span class="iast">viṣka</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0486</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">sakarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">दर्शने</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(darśane)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to see</span>
    </div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0487">
  <div class="skt-header-line">
    <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षिप</span>
    <span class="iast">kṣipa</span>
    <span class="skt-id">#10.0487</span>
    <div class="skt-badges">
      <span class="badge badge-pada">ubhayapada</span>
      <span class="badge badge-settva">seṭ</span>
      <span class="badge badge-karma">akarmaka</span>
      <span class="badge badge-page" title="Historischer Druck Seite 106">S. 106</span>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="skt-body">
    <div class="skt-meaning">
      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">प्रेरणे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(preraṇe)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to inspire</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">क्षिप</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2574</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> अदन्तः । अयं धातुः क्षीरस्वामिनः धातुवृत्तिकारस्य च न सम्मतः इति भाति, उभयोः ग्रन्थयोः अपाठात् । कौमुद्यां तु पाठः दृश्यते । &#x27; क्षप &#x27; इति मा.धा.वृत्तौ ।</div>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="skt-entry" id="entry-10.0488">

      <span class="deva-artha" lang="sa">निवासे</span>
      <span class="iast-artha">(nivāse)</span>
      <span class="en-artha">— to live, to dwell, to stay</span>
    </div>
    <div class="skt-meta"><span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">Upadeśa:</span> <span class="deva" lang="sa">वस</span></span> <span class="meta-item"><span class="meta-label">SK:</span> 2574</span> <span class="meta-item meta-tags">उदात्तोपदेशः · नित्यणिच्</span></div>
    <div class="skt-commentary"><span class="comm-label">Kommentar:</span> &#x27; वस्यति स्तम्भने, वस्ते छादने वसयेत् वसेत् । निवासे वासयेत्, स्नेहे वासयत्युपसेवने &#x27; (श्लो. 187 - 188) इति देवः ।</div>
  </div>
</div>
